Well, I try to stay active, I really do. I don't want to be the kind of guy who just sits around and complains. But... I suppose if I'm being completely honest—and I want to make sure I'm giving you the right information here—for a long time now, there's just been this... this sort of gray, flat feeling most days.

I feel like I might be repeating myself a little, and I'm so sorry if I'm not giving you the exact answer you need. I really want to be helpful here.

I hope I'm not coming across as too negative or difficult. Oh, wow. Um, I am so sorry, I feel terrible that you have to keep asking me this. I must really be missing the mark, and I hate to be difficult or slow things down for you.
